Fan out in parallel with security-auditor, performance-auditor, and test-quality-auditor. Read-only. NEVER fixes anything. Reads `.claude/project.json` + Layer 1 (if configured) + Layer 2 (state/*.md) + Layer 3 (code) live on every invocation.
